Hi, Doctor. Thank you so much for seeing me. Um, I hope I'm explaining this right, but... I came in because I've been struggling a bit at work lately. 

I'm a production manager, and usually, I try to be a really dedicated employee. Like, recently I was launching all these nonstop plant redesign projects, and I was calling our suppliers after hours just to get ahead, and I even rewrote the whole shift plan entirely by myself. I really just wanted to do a great job for the company and make sure everything was running smoothly, you know? 

But... um... lately, I've just suddenly found myself completely unable to keep up with the very plans I had just set in motion. It's actually really embarrassing to admit that I'm dropping the ball now after taking on so much. I feel terrible about it. I just want to get back to being a good, reliable manager. Is... is that the kind of thing you can help me with?

Um, well... I guess I still try to enjoy things, you know? I mean, I really don't want to sound ungrateful or like I'm just giving up on everything.

I really just wanted to do a great job for the company and make sure everything was running smoothly, you know? \n\nBut... um... lately, I've just suddenly found myself completely unable to keep up with the very plans I had just set in motion. It's actually really embarrassing to admit that I'm dropping the ball now after taking on so much. I feel terrible about it. I just want to get back to being a good, reliable manager. Is... is that the kind of thing you can help me with?\n\nDoctor: Have you been worrying excessively about things beyond just your work performance?\n\nPatient: Um, no... I don't think so. I mean, I hope I'm understanding you right, but I don't really worry about other things excessively. I try to be a pretty positive person, usually. \n\nIt's really just the work stuff right now, mostly because I just hate feeling like I'm letting my team down. I just want to be reliable for them. But outside of the plant, I don't really sit around stressing about other parts of my life. I try to keep everything else pretty manageable. Does... does that make sense? I hope I'm giving you the right kind of information.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or down most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: Um, well... I guess I have been feeling a little down. I mean, I don't want to sound like I'm complaining or anything, because I know I have a lot to be thankful for. But... lately, I wake up in my temporary apartment and there's just this... this heavy, hollow feeling. \n\nIt's like... it's like the day is already ruined before I even drive to the plant, if that makes sense. I try really hard to shake it off, I really do, because I know I need to be upbeat for the team. But it's just been lingering there. Is that... is that normal for someone going through a rough patch at work? I hope I'm not overreacting by feeling this way.\n\nDoctor: Have you been enjoying activities that you usually find pleasurable?\n\nPatient: Um, well... I guess I still try to enjoy things, you know? I mean, I really don't want to sound ungrateful or like I'm just giving up on everything. \n\nNormally, I love hiking on the weekends, or even just trying out new recipes. But lately... um, I don't know if I'm just tired from everything at work, but I haven't really had the energy to do those things. Like, I'll think about going for a walk, but then I just kind of sit on the couch instead. I mean, I still *want* to like those things, I just... haven't been doing them as much. \n\nI hope that doesn't make me sound lazy. I really do try to stay active when I can. Is it okay that I'm just feeling a little too worn out for my hobbies right now?\n\nDoctor: Have you been experiencing any physical symptoms like rapid heartbeat, sweating, or trembling lately?\n\nPatient: Um, no... I haven't really noticed anything like that. I mean, my heart feels fine, and I definitely haven't been trembling or anything. I promise I did. But then with the move to the temporary apartment, and taking on this new assignment at the plant... it's just made it a lot harder to manage everything. I didn't want to make a fuss because I know everyone gets stressed with relocations and new roles, you know? \n\nDo you think three months is... I mean, is it okay that it's taking me this long to adjust? Um, well... no, not really. I mean, I definitely haven't just stopped caring about my friends or anything like that! I really hope it doesn't sound like I'm isolating myself. I know how important it is to keep up with people.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um, I wouldn't say *every* single day. I really don't want you to think I'm just miserable all the time, because I'm definitely not! I mean, I have days where I feel totally fine, especially if things go well at the plant or if I get a good night's sleep. 

I guess it's just... it's been more days than not lately where I have that heavy feeling I mentioned? But I really try not to let it take over the whole week. Like, if I have a bad Tuesday, I make sure I push myself extra hard on Wednesday to have a good day.

Um, well... I guess it's been a little while now. I really hope it doesn't sound like I've just been letting things slide, because I really have been trying to push through it.

Um, energy... well, I wouldn't say I'm fatigued every single day. I really want to make sure I'm being clear that I'm still getting my job done! I'm definitely not just lying in bed all day or anything like that.
